FILE: lib\master-data.ts

export type DayKey = 'sun' | 'mon' | 'tue' | 'wed' | 'thu' | 'fri' | 'sat'
export type DayName = 'Sunday' | 'Monday' | 'Tuesday' | 'Wednesday' | 'Thursday' | 'Friday' | 'Saturday'

export interface MergedSessionPart {
  subjectCode: string
  subjectTitle: string
  grade: string
  teacher: string
  students?: number
}

export interface MasterRoutineItem {
  id: string
  day: DayName
  dayKey: DayKey
  timeSlot: string
  slotId: string
  span?: number
  subjectCode: string
  subjectTitle: string
  grade: string
  gradeKey: 'class-6' | 'class-7' | 'class-8' | 'class-9' | 'class-10' | 'class-11' | 'class-12'
  teacher: string
  teacherId?: string | null
  lab: 'Computer Lab' | 'Physics Lab' | 'Chemistry Lab' | 'Biology Lab' | 'Electronics Lab'
  labKey: 'comp' | 'phys' | 'chem' | 'bio' | 'elec'
  labCode?: string
  defaultStudents: number
  category: 'Computer' | 'Physics' | 'Chemistry' | 'Biology' | 'Electronics' | 'General'
  dotColor: string
  accentColor: string
  badgeColor: string
  mergedParts?: [MergedSessionPart, MergedSessionPart]
  secondaryLab?: string
  secondaryLabKey?: 'comp' | 'phys' | 'chem' | 'bio' | 'elec'
  isDualLab?: boolean
  coTeacher?: string
  classSubjectId?: string | null
  academicYearId?: string | null
  status?: 'confirmed' | 'requested' | 'skipped'
  isSkipped?: boolean
  skippedReason?: string
  skippedBy?: string
  requestedBy?: string
  declineReason?: string
  date?: string
  specificDate?: string | null
  isAdHoc?: boolean
}

export function isLabBusyInSlot(
  routines: MasterRoutineItem[],
  dayKey: DayKey,
  slotId: string,
  labKey: string,
  excludeSessionId?: string
): boolean {
  if (!routines || !dayKey || !slotId || !labKey) return false
  const normKey = labKey.toLowerCase().trim()
  return routines.some((s) => {
    if (excludeSessionId && s.id === excludeSessionId) return false
    if (s.dayKey !== dayKey || s.slotId !== slotId) return false
    if (s.isSkipped || s.status === 'skipped') return false
    const primaryMatch = (s.labKey || '').toLowerCase().trim() === normKey
    const secondaryMatch = Boolean(s.isDualLab && (s.secondaryLabKey || '').toLowerCase().trim() === normKey)
    return primaryMatch || secondaryMatch
  })
}

export function isTeacherBusyInSlot(
  routines: MasterRoutineItem[],
  dayKey: DayKey,
  slotId: string,
  teacherName: string,
  excludeSessionId?: string
): boolean {
  if (!routines || !dayKey || !slotId || !teacherName) return false
  const normTeacher = teacherName.toLowerCase().trim()
  return routines.some((s) => {
    if (excludeSessionId && s.id === excludeSessionId) return false
    if (s.dayKey !== dayKey || s.slotId !== slotId) return false
    if (s.isSkipped || s.status === 'skipped') return false
    const sTeacher = (s.teacher || '').toLowerCase().trim()
    const sCoTeacher = s.isDualLab ? (s.coTeacher || '').toLowerCase().trim() : ''
    return sTeacher === normTeacher || (sCoTeacher !== '' && sCoTeacher === normTeacher)
  })
}

export function getOccupiedLabsInSlot(
  routines: MasterRoutineItem[],
  dayKey: DayKey,
  slotId: string,
  excludeSessionId?: string,
  periodSlots?: Array<{ id: string }>
): Set<string> {
  const occupied = new Set<string>()
  if (!routines || !dayKey || !slotId) return occupied

  const parseSlotOrder = (id: string) => {
    if (periodSlots && periodSlots.length > 0) {
      const idx = periodSlots.findIndex((t) => t.id === id)
      if (idx !== -1) return idx
    }
    const match = id.match(/\d+/)
    return match ? parseInt(match[0], 10) : -1
  }

  const targetSlotIdx = parseSlotOrder(slotId)
  if (targetSlotIdx === -1) return occupied

  routines.forEach((s) => {
    if (excludeSessionId && s.id === excludeSessionId) return
    if (s.dayKey !== dayKey) return
    if (s.isSkipped || s.status === 'skipped') return

    const sSlotIdx = parseSlotOrder(s.slotId)
    if (sSlotIdx === -1) return
    const sSpan = s.span || 1

    if (targetSlotIdx >= sSlotIdx && targetSlotIdx < sSlotIdx + sSpan) {
      if (s.labKey) occupied.add(s.labKey.toLowerCase().trim())
      if (s.isDualLab && s.secondaryLabKey) occupied.add(s.secondaryLabKey.toLowerCase().trim())
    }
  })
  return occupied
}

export interface HolidayItem {
  id: string
  title: string
  name?: string
  titleNp?: string
  dateStr: string // YYYY-MM-DD (Start Date)
  startDateNp?: string
  endDateStr?: string // YYYY-MM-DD (End Date for multi-day vacations)
  endDateNp?: string
  bsDateStr?: string // e.g. "à¥¨à¥¦à¥®à¥© à¤…à¤¸à¥‹à¤œ à¥¨à¥ª - à¤•à¤¾à¤¤à¥à¤¤à¤¿à¤• à¥«"
  type: 'state' | 'cultural' | 'department' | 'weekend' | 'vacation'
  description?: string
}

export function isDateWithinHoliday(dateInput: string | Date, holiday: HolidayItem): boolean {
  if (!holiday || !holiday.dateStr) return false
  const dStr = typeof dateInput === 'string' ? dateInput.split('T')[0].trim() : getNepalDateStr(dateInput)
  const start = (holiday.dateStr || '').trim()
  const end = (holiday.endDateStr || start).trim()
  const [minDate, maxDate] = start <= end ? [start, end] : [end, start]
  return dStr >= minDate && dStr <= maxDate
}

import { parseSlotTimeRange } from '@/lib/utils'

export const CANONICAL_PERIOD_SLOTS = [
  { id: 't1', label: '09:15 - 10:00', name: 'Pre-Period' },
  { id: 't2', label: '10:10 - 11:00', name: 'Period 1' },
  { id: 't3', label: '11:00 - 11:45', name: 'Period 2' },
  { id: 't4', label: '11:45 - 12:30', name: 'Period 3' },
  { id: 't5', label: '12:30 - 01:15', name: 'Period 4' },
  { id: 't6', label: '01:15 - 01:45', name: 'Break / Tiffin' },
  { id: 't7', label: '01:45 - 02:30', name: 'Period 5' },
  { id: 't8', label: '02:30 - 03:15', name: 'Period 6' },
  { id: 't9', label: '03:15 - 04:05', name: 'Period 7' },
  { id: 't10', label: '04:05 - 04:50', name: 'Period 8' },
]

export function computeCombinedTimeRange(
  slotId: string,
  span: number = 1,
  slotList?: Array<{ id: string; label: string; name?: string }>
): string {
  const effectiveSlots = slotList && slotList.length > 0 ? slotList : CANONICAL_PERIOD_SLOTS

  const startIdx = effectiveSlots.findIndex((t) => t.id === slotId)
  if (startIdx === -1) return '10:10 - 11:00'
  const startSlot = effectiveSlots[startIdx]
  const startTime = parseSlotTimeRange(startSlot.label).startTime

  const endIdx = Math.min(startIdx + span - 1, effectiveSlots.length - 1)
  const endSlot = effectiveSlots[endIdx]
  const endTime = parseSlotTimeRange(endSlot.label).endTime

  return `${startTime} - ${endTime}`
}

import { getNepaliDate, toNepaliDigits, getNepalDateStr } from '@/lib/nepali-date'

export function getOrderedDays(startDay: 'sun' | 'mon' = 'sun') {
  if (startDay === 'mon') {
    return [
      DAYS.find((d) => d.id === 'mon')!,
      DAYS.find((d) => d.id === 'tue')!,
      DAYS.find((d) => d.id === 'wed')!,
      DAYS.find((d) => d.id === 'thu')!,
      DAYS.find((d) => d.id === 'fri')!,
      DAYS.find((d) => d.id === 'sat')!,
      DAYS.find((d) => d.id === 'sun')!,
    ]
  }
  return [...DAYS]
}

export function getWeekDates(baseDate: Date = new Date(), startDay: 'sun' | 'mon' = 'sun') {
  const currentDay = baseDate.getDay() // 0 is Sunday, 1 is Monday ... 6 is Saturday
  const startDayOffset = startDay === 'mon' ? (currentDay === 0 ? 6 : currentDay - 1) : currentDay
  const startDate = new Date(baseDate)
  startDate.setDate(baseDate.getDate() - startDayOffset)

  const orderedDays = getOrderedDays(startDay)

  return orderedDays.map((d, index) => {
    const dayDate = new Date(startDate)
    dayDate.setDate(startDate.getDate() + index)
    const dateStr = getNepalDateStr(dayDate)
    const dayNum = dayDate.getDate()
    const monthName = dayDate.toLocaleDateString('en-US', { month: 'short' })
    const nepaliInfo = getNepaliDate(dayDate)

    return {
      dayKey: d.id,
      dateStr,
      formattedEng: `${dayNum} ${monthName}`,
      formattedNp: `${toNepaliDigits(nepaliInfo.bsDay)} ${nepaliInfo.bsMonthNameNp}`, // e.g. "à¥§à¥­ à¤­à¤¦à¥Œ"
      nepaliFull: nepaliInfo.formattedDateNp,
      isWeekend: d.isWeekend,
    }
  })
}

export function formatGradeBadge(grade: string, subjectCode?: string): string {
  if (!grade) return '12C'
  let clean = grade.replace(/Class\s*/i, '').trim()
  if (clean === '12') {
    if (subjectCode?.includes('COMP')) return '12C'
    if (subjectCode?.includes('CHEM') || subjectCode?.includes('PHY')) return '12 Sc'
    if (subjectCode?.includes('MGT')) return '12 Mgt'
    return '12C'
  }
  if (clean === '11') {
    if (subjectCode?.includes('COMP')) return '11C'
    if (subjectCode?.includes('MGT')) return '11 Mgt'
    return '11 Sc'
  }
  if (clean === '10') return '10A'
  if (clean === '9') return '9B'
  if (clean === '8') return '8A'
  if (clean === '7') return '7B'
  if (clean === '6') return '6A'
  return clean
}

export function formatCleanSubjectCode(subjectCode: string, grade?: string): string {
  if (!subjectCode) return 'PRAC'
  // If already contains sub-class suffix like -12C, -11SC, -10A, -7C, -6A, return uppercase
  if (/-[0-9]+[A-Za-z]+$/i.test(subjectCode)) {
    return subjectCode.toUpperCase()
  }
  if (grade) {
    const cleanBadge = formatGradeBadge(grade, subjectCode)
    const normalizedBadge = cleanBadge.replace(/\s+/g, '').toUpperCase()
    const match = subjectCode.match(/^([A-Za-z]+)[-\s]?([0-9]+)?/i)
    if (match) {
      const prefix = match[1].toUpperCase()
      return `${prefix}-${normalizedBadge}`
    }
  }
  return subjectCode.toUpperCase()
}

/**
 * Strips any redundant subject code or prefix from the subject title.
 * e.g. "BIO-12 â€“ Cell Biology & Genetics Lab" -> "Cell Biology & Genetics Lab"
 */
export function cleanSubjectTitle(subjectCode?: string, subjectTitle?: string): string {
  if (!subjectTitle) return ''
  const trimmed = subjectTitle.trim()
  if (!subjectCode) return trimmed

  const cleanCode = formatCleanSubjectCode(subjectCode)
  const escapedRaw = subjectCode.replace(/[-/\\^$*+?.()|[\]{}]/g, '\\$&')
  const escapedClean = cleanCode.replace(/[-/\\^$*+?.()|[\]{}]/g, '\\$&')
  const pattern = new RegExp(`^(${escapedRaw}|${escapedClean})\\s*[-â€“â€”:]\\s*`, 'i')

  const stripped = trimmed.replace(pattern, '').trim()
  return stripped || trimmed
}

/**
 * Generates an elegant, non-repeating display heading for a subject session card.
 * e.g. "BIO-12 â€” Cell Biology & Genetics Lab".
 * Never duplicates like "BIO-12 â€” BIO-12 â€“ Cell Biology".
 */
export function formatSubjectHeading(subjectCode?: string, subjectTitle?: string): string {
  const code = formatCleanSubjectCode(subjectCode || '')
  if (!subjectTitle) return code || 'Practical Session'
  const cleanTitle = cleanSubjectTitle(subjectCode, subjectTitle)
  if (!code) return cleanTitle
  if (cleanTitle.toLowerCase() === code.toLowerCase()) return code
  return `${code} â€” ${cleanTitle}`
}

/**
 * Formats a clean, canonical label for a class batch dropdown option.
 * Strictly presents the grade and section without misleading room capacity numbers,
 * stream names, or the literal word 'section'.
 * E.g. { name: "12 Eng", section: "Eng" } -> "12 Eng"
 * E.g. { name: "12", section: "Eng" } -> "12 Eng"
 * E.g. { name: "8A", section: "A" } -> "8A"
 */
export function formatClassBatchLabel(cls?: { name?: string; section?: string | null } | null): string {
  if (!cls) return ''
  const name = (cls.name || '').trim()
  const rawSection = (cls.section || '').trim()
  const cleanSection = rawSection.replace(/^(?:section|sec)[\s.:_-]*/i, '').trim()

  if (!name) return cleanSection
  if (!cleanSection) return name

  // If name already contains the clean section (e.g. "12 Eng" contains "Eng", or "8A" contains "A")
  const regex = new RegExp(`\\b${cleanSection.replace(/[-/\\^$*+?.()|[\]{}]/g, '\\$&')}\\b`, 'i')
  if (regex.test(name) || name.toLowerCase().endsWith(cleanSection.toLowerCase())) {
    return name
  }

  return `${name} ${cleanSection}`
}

export interface ClassBatchItem {
  id: string
  name: string // e.g. "12C"
  fullName: string // e.g. "12C - Tech Stream Sec A"
  stream: string
  strength: number
}

export interface SubjectItem {
  code: string
  title: string
  fullName: string
  grade: string
  lab: string
  labId: string
  teacherId: string
  defaultTopic?: string
  quota: number
}

export function computeMultiPeriodLabel(
  periodIds: string[],
  slotList?: Array<{ id: string; label: string; name?: string }>
): { label: string; timeRange: string } {
  if (!periodIds || periodIds.length === 0) {
    return { label: 'Period 1 (10:10 - 11:00)', timeRange: '10:10 - 11:00' }
  }

  const effectiveSlots = slotList && slotList.length > 0 ? slotList : CANONICAL_PERIOD_SLOTS

  const sorted = [...periodIds].sort((a, b) => {
    if (effectiveSlots && effectiveSlots.length > 0) {
      const aIdx = effectiveSlots.findIndex((p) => p.id === a)
      const bIdx = effectiveSlots.findIndex((p) => p.id === b)
      if (aIdx !== -1 && bIdx !== -1) return aIdx - bIdx
    }
    const aNum = parseInt(a.replace(/\D/g, ''), 10) || 0
    const bNum = parseInt(b.replace(/\D/g, ''), 10) || 0
    return aNum - bNum
  })

  const firstSlot = effectiveSlots?.find((p) => p.id === sorted[0])
  const lastSlot = effectiveSlots?.find((p) => p.id === sorted[sorted.length - 1]) || firstSlot

  const startTime = firstSlot ? parseSlotTimeRange(firstSlot.label).startTime : '10:10'
  const endTime = lastSlot ? parseSlotTimeRange(lastSlot.label).endTime : '11:00'

  const names = sorted
    .map((id) => {
      const slot = effectiveSlots?.find((p) => p.id === id)
      return slot ? slot.name : id
    })
    .join(' & ')

  return {
    label: `${names} (${startTime} - ${endTime})`,
    timeRange: `${startTime} - ${endTime}`,
  }
}

export const GRADE_STRENGTH: Record<string, number> = {
  '12C': 38,
  '12 Sc': 40,
  '12 Mgt': 36,
  '11C': 42,
  '11 Sc': 38,
  '10A': 35,
  '9B': 36,
  '8A': 32,
  '7B': 30,
  '6A': 28,
  'Class 12': 38,
  'Class 11': 42,
  'Class 10': 35,
  'Class 9': 36,
  'Class 8': 32,
  'Class 7': 30,
  'Class 6': 28,
}

/**
 * Fuzzy check if two class/grade identifiers belong to the same level / cohort
 * e.g. "12 Eng" matches "12", "Class 12", "12 Eng", "12C", "12 Sc"
 */
export function matchesGrade(classOrGradeA?: string | null, classOrGradeB?: string | null): boolean {
  if (!classOrGradeA || !classOrGradeB) return true
  const normA = classOrGradeA.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/^class\s+/i, '')
  const normB = classOrGradeB.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/^class\s+/i, '')
  
  if (normA === normB) return true

  const cleanA = normA.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '')
  const cleanB = normB.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '')
  if (cleanA === cleanB) return true

  const numA = normA.match(/\d+/)?.[0]
  const numB = normB.match(/\d+/)?.[0]

  if (numA && numB && numA !== numB) return false

  const suffixA = cleanA.replace(/^\d+/, '')
  const suffixB = cleanB.replace(/^\d+/, '')

  // If both have explicit section/stream suffixes, they must match
  if (suffixA && suffixB) {
    if (suffixA === suffixB) return true
    if (suffixA.length === 1 && suffixB.length === 1 && suffixA !== suffixB) {
      return false
    }
    const isMgtA = suffixA.startsWith('mg') || suffixA === 'm'
    const isMgtB = suffixB.startsWith('mg') || suffixB === 'm'
    const isSciA = suffixA.startsWith('sc') || suffixA === 's' || suffixA === 'c'
    const isSciB = suffixB.startsWith('sc') || suffixB === 's' || suffixB === 'c'
    if ((isMgtA && isSciB) || (isMgtB && isSciA)) return false
    return suffixA.startsWith(suffixB) || suffixB.startsWith(suffixA)
  }

  if (numA && numB && numA === numB) {
    return true
  }

  return false
}

import { matchesGrade } from '@/lib/master-data'
import type { UserScopeContext } from '@/lib/context/user-scope'

export interface CascadingSelections {
  labId?: string
  classBatch?: string
  subjectCode?: string
  teacherId?: string
}

export interface CascadingOptions {
  validLabs: Array<{ id: string; name: string }>
  validClasses: Array<{ id: string; name: string; fullName: string }>
  validSubjects: Array<{ code: string; title: string; grade: string; labId: string; labName: string }>
  validTeachers: Array<{ id: string; name: string; email: string }>
}

export interface CascadingCustomData {
  classes?: Array<{ id: string; name: string; stream?: string; capacity?: number }>
  subjects?: Array<{ code: string; title: string; grade: string; labId?: string; lab?: string; teacherId?: string; teacherName?: string }>
  labs?: Array<{ id: string; name: string }>
  teachers?: Array<{ id: string; name?: string; full_name?: string; email?: string }>
}

/**
 * Pure calculation of dynamic cascading options based on current user scope and active selections.
 * For privileged users (super_admin, hod, lab_incharge), allows all rows returned from PostgreSQL directly.
 */
export function getCascadingOptions(
  scope: UserScopeContext,
  selections: CascadingSelections = {},
  customData?: CascadingCustomData
): CascadingOptions {
  const { labId, classBatch, subjectCode, teacherId } = selections

  // 1. Initial base set based on user role and live PostgreSQL customData
  let baseTeachers = customData?.teachers && customData.teachers.length > 0
    ? customData.teachers.map((t) => ({ id: t.id, name: t.name || t.full_name || 'Faculty', email: t.email || '' }))
    : scope.isPrivileged
    ? []
    : [{ id: scope.userId || 'self', name: scope.fullName, email: scope.email }]

  let rawLabs = (customData?.labs && customData.labs.length > 0)
    ? customData.labs
    : (!scope.isPrivileged && scope.assignedLabIds && scope.assignedLabIds.length > 0)
    ? scope.assignedLabIds.map((id) => ({ id, name: id.toUpperCase() }))
    : []
  let baseLabs = scope.isPrivileged ? rawLabs : rawLabs.filter((l) => scope.assignedLabIds.includes(l.id))

  let rawClasses = (customData?.classes && customData.classes.length > 0)
    ? customData.classes.map((c) => ({
        id: c.id,
        name: c.name,
        fullName: c.stream ? `${c.name} (${c.stream})` : c.name,
      }))
    : (!scope.isPrivileged && scope.assignedClasses && scope.assignedClasses.length > 0)
    ? scope.assignedClasses.map((clsName) => ({
        id: clsName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''),
        name: clsName,
        fullName: clsName,
      }))
    : []
  let baseClasses = scope.isPrivileged ? rawClasses : rawClasses.filter((c) => scope.assignedClasses.includes(c.name))

  let rawSubjects = (customData?.subjects && customData.subjects.length > 0)
    ? customData.subjects.map((s: any) => ({
        code: s.code,
        title: s.title || s.name || s.code,
        grade: s.grade,
        labId: s.labId || 'comp',
        labName: s.lab || 'Laboratory',
      }))
    : (!scope.isPrivileged && scope.assignedSubjects && scope.assignedSubjects.length > 0)
    ? scope.assignedSubjects
    : []
  let baseSubjects = scope.isPrivileged ? rawSubjects : (scope.assignedSubjects.length > 0 ? scope.assignedSubjects : rawSubjects)

  // 2. Cascade by teacher selection
  const activeTeacherId = (!scope.isPrivileged ? scope.fullName : teacherId) || ''
  if (activeTeacherId && activeTeacherId !== 'all') {
    if (customData?.subjects && customData.subjects.length > 0) {
      const teacherSubs = customData.subjects.filter(
        (s: any) => s.teacherId === activeTeacherId || s.teacherName === activeTeacherId
      )
      if (teacherSubs.length > 0) {
        baseSubjects = teacherSubs.map((s: any) => ({
          code: s.code,
          title: s.title || s.name || s.code,
          grade: s.grade,
          labId: s.labId || 'comp',
          labName: s.lab || 'Laboratory',
        }))
        baseClasses = baseClasses.filter((c) =>
          teacherSubs.some((ts: any) => matchesGrade(ts.grade, c.name))
        )
      }
    }
  }

  // 3. Cascade by Lab selection
  if (labId && labId !== 'all') {
    const labFilteredSubs = baseSubjects.filter((s) => s.labId === labId)
    if (labFilteredSubs.length > 0) {
      baseSubjects = labFilteredSubs
      baseClasses = baseClasses.filter((c) =>
        labFilteredSubs.some((s) => matchesGrade(s.grade, c.name))
      )
    }
  }

  // 4. Cascade by Class/Grade selection
  if (classBatch && classBatch !== 'all') {
    const classFilteredSubs = baseSubjects.filter((s) => matchesGrade(s.grade, classBatch))
    if (classFilteredSubs.length > 0) {
      baseSubjects = classFilteredSubs
      const validLabsInClass = new Set(classFilteredSubs.map((s) => s.labId))
      baseLabs = baseLabs.filter((l) => validLabsInClass.has(l.id))
    }
  }

  // 5. Cascade by Subject selection
  if (subjectCode && subjectCode !== 'all') {
    const cleanSubCode = subjectCode.split(/[\s-â€”]/)[0].toUpperCase()
    const targetSub = baseSubjects.find((s) => s.code.toUpperCase().startsWith(cleanSubCode))
    if (targetSub) {
      baseLabs = baseLabs.filter((l) => l.id === targetSub.labId)
      baseClasses = baseClasses.filter((c) => matchesGrade(c.name, targetSub.grade))
    }
  }

  return {
    validLabs: baseLabs.map((l) => ({ id: l.id, name: l.name })),
    validClasses: baseClasses,
    validSubjects: baseSubjects,
    validTeachers: baseTeachers,
  }
}



=========================================
FILE: lib\context\server-user-scope.ts
=========================================

import { createClient } from '../supabase/server'
import { isSupabaseConfigured } from '../supabase/config'
import { getCurrentUserProfile } from '@/app/actions/auth'
import { resolveUserScope, type UserScopeContext } from './user-scope'

export type { UserScopeContext }

/**
 * Server-side helper to get the authenticated user's scope context,
 * augmented with real-time class_subjects database assignments.
 * 
 * STRICT APPARATUS / APP ROUTER ISOLATION:
 * This function imports createClient from @/lib/supabase/server which uses 'next/headers'.
 * It must remain strictly in this server-only file and NEVER be bundled into Client Components.
 */
export async function getServerUserScope(): Promise<UserScopeContext> {
  const profile = await getCurrentUserProfile()
  const baseScope = resolveUserScope(profile)

  // For approved teachers, query live class_subjects database records directly from PostgreSQL
  // Deprecating hardcoded INSTITUTIONAL_TEACHERS fallback when database is configured
  if (profile && profile.role === 'teacher' && profile.approval_status === 'approved' && Boolean((profile as any)?.is_active ?? true)) {
    try {
      if (isSupabaseConfigured()) {
        const supabase = await createClient()
        const { data: dbAssignments, error: dbErr } = await supabase
          .from('class_subjects')
          .select(`
            class_id,
            subject_id,
            default_lab_id,
            classes:class_id(name),
            subjects:subject_id(code, name)
          `)
          .eq('teacher_id', profile.id)

        if (!dbErr) {
          const assignedClasses = new Set<string>()
          const assignedLabs = new Set<string>()
          const assignedSubjects: UserScopeContext['assignedSubjects'] = []

          for (const row of (dbAssignments || []) as any[]) {
            const className = row.classes?.name || row.class_id
            if (className) assignedClasses.add(className)
            if (row.default_lab_id) assignedLabs.add(row.default_lab_id)
            if (row.subjects) {
              const code = row.subjects.code || row.subject_id
              if (!assignedSubjects.some((s) => s.code === code && s.grade === className)) {
                assignedSubjects.push({
                  code,
                  title: row.subjects.name || code,
                  grade: className,
                  labId: row.default_lab_id || 'comp',
                  labName: 'Laboratory',
                })
              }
            }
          }

          return {
            ...baseScope,
            assignedClasses: Array.from(assignedClasses),
            assignedLabIds: Array.from(assignedLabs),
            assignedSubjects,
            teacherProfile: null, // Deprecated INSTITUTIONAL_TEACHERS in favor of canonical PostgreSQL truth
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Error querying teacher scope from class_subjects:', err)
    }
  }

  return baseScope
}

import type { RecordReportSpec } from '../../types/report-spec'

/**
 * Sanitizes subject names to defensively strip repeating course code prefixes.
 * E.g. "COMP-12 - COMP-12 â€” Data Structures & Algorithms Lab" -> "COMP-12 â€” Data Structures & Algorithms Lab"
 * E.g. "COMP-12 - Data Structures & Algorithms Lab" -> "Data Structures & Algorithms Lab"
 */
export function sanitizeSubjectTitle(subject: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!subject || !subject.trim()) return 'â€”'
  let s = subject.trim()
  // Clean repeating code prefix: "COMP-12 - COMP-12 â€” ..." -> "COMP-12 â€” ..."
  s = s.replace(/^([A-Z0-9-]+)\s*[-â€“â€”]\s*\1\s*[-â€“â€”]\s*/i, '$1 â€” ')
  return s
}

/**
 * Canonical Class Alias Map
 * Maps canonical institutional classes from the Admin Panel to known historical/legacy variations.
 */
export const CLASS_ALIAS_MAP: Record<string, string[]> = {
  '12 Eng': ['12 Eng', '12C', '12C - Tech Stream Sec A', '12C - Tech Stream', '12-Eng', 'Class 12', '12C-A'],
  '11 Eng': ['11 Eng', '11 Sc', '11 Sc - Science Stream Sec B', '11 Sc - Science Sec B', '11-Eng', 'Class 11', '11Sc-B', '11Sc'],
  '12 Mgt': ['12 Mgt', '12 Mgt - Mgmt Stream Sec A', '12-Mgt'],
  '11 Mgt': ['11 Mgt', '11C - Computer Tech Sec A', '11C', '11-Mgt'],
  '10 Eng': ['10 Eng', 'Class 10', 'Grade 10'],
  '9 Eng': ['9 Eng', 'Class 9', 'Grade 9'],
  '8A': ['8A', 'Grade 8A'],
  '7B': ['7B', 'Grade 7B'],
  '6A': ['6A', 'Grade 6A'],
}

/**
 * Resolves a class name into all known query aliases for backward compatibility.
 */
export function getClassAliases(className: string | null | undefined): string[] {
  if (!className || className === 'all') return []
  return CLASS_ALIAS_MAP[className] || [className]
}

/**
 * Shortens institutional class strings to clean codes.
 * E.g. "12 Eng - Computer Engineering" -> "12 Eng", "12C - Tech Stream Sec A" -> "12C-A".
 * Explicit Fallback: Returns raw original string verbatim if unrecognized.
 */
export function formatClassCode(batchGroup: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!batchGroup || !batchGroup.trim()) return 'â€”'
  const clean = batchGroup.trim()

  // 1. Canonical Admin Panel Classes: e.g. "12 Eng - Computer Engineering" -> "12 Eng", "8A - General Science" -> "8A"
  const matchCanonical = clean.match(/^(\d+\s*(?:Eng|Mgt)|[6789][A-Z]|\d+)\s*-\s*[A-Za-z\s&-]+$/i)
  if (matchCanonical) {
    return matchCanonical[1].trim()
  }

  // Exact match for already-canonical class names: "12 Eng", "11 Eng", "12 Mgt", "8A", "7B", "6A"
  if (/^(\d+\s*(?:Eng|Mgt)|[6789][A-Z])$/i.test(clean)) {
    return clean
  }

  // 2. Grade + Stream + Section: "12C - Tech Stream Sec A" -> "12C-A", "11 Sc - Science Stream Sec B" -> "11Sc-B"
  const matchSec = clean.match(/^(\d+[A-Za-z\s]+?)(?:\s*-\s*[A-Za-z\s-]+)?(?:\s+(?:Sec|Section)\s+([A-Za-z0-9]+))$/i)
  if (matchSec) {
    const base = matchSec[1].replace(/\s+/g, '').trim()
    const sec = matchSec[2].trim()
    return `${base}-${sec}`
  }

  // 3. Grade format: "Grade 11 - Sci A" -> "11-SciA", "Grade 12 - Sci B" -> "12-SciB"
  const matchGrade = clean.match(/^Grade\s+(\d+)\s*-\s*([A-Za-z0-9\s-]+)$/i)
  if (matchGrade) {
    return `${matchGrade[1]}-${matchGrade[2].replace(/\s+/g, '')}`
  }

  // 4. Simple class format: "Class 10", "Class 11", "Class 6" -> "Class 10", "Class 11"
  if (/^Class\s+\d+$/i.test(clean)) {
    return clean
  }

  // 5. Grade + Stream without section: "12B - Bio-Science Stream" -> "12B", "11 Sc - Science Stream" -> "11Sc"
  const matchStream = clean.match(/^(\d+[A-Za-z\s]*?)\s*-\s*[A-Za-z\s-]+(?:Stream)?$/i)
  if (matchStream) {
    return matchStream[1].replace(/\s+/g, '').trim()
  }

  // 6. Short code already: "12C", "11A" -> return as-is
  if (/^\d+[A-Za-z]{1,2}$/.test(clean)) {
    return clean
  }

  // EXPLICIT FALLBACK: Return raw original string verbatim, never mangled!
  return clean
}

/**
 * Formats subject code to strictly isolate the subject code without the class suffix.
 * E.g. "WMAD-11" -> "WMAD", "COMP-12" -> "COMP", "CHEM-11" -> "CHEM", "CP-9" -> "CP".
 */
export function cleanSubjectCode(code?: string | null, name?: string | null): string {
  if (code && code.trim()) {
    return code.trim().replace(/[-_\s]+(?:Class\s*)?\d+[A-Za-z]?$/i, '').trim()
  }
  if (name && name.trim()) {
    const codeMatch = name.match(/\b([A-Z]{2,6})(?:[-_\s]*\d+)?\b/)
    if (codeMatch) return codeMatch[1]
    return name.slice(0, 6).toUpperCase()
  }
  return 'â€”'
}

/**
 * Strips course code and class prefixes from the practical title.
 * E.g. "WMAD-11 Practical Session" -> "Practical Session"
 * E.g. "CHEM-11 - Chemistry Lab" -> "Chemistry Lab"
 * E.g. "COMP-12 Practical Session" -> "Practical Session"
 * E.g. "PHY-12 - Physics Lab" -> "Physics Lab"
 * E.g. "CP-9 - Computer Prog" -> "Computer Prog"
 */
export function cleanPracticalTitle(title: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!title || !title.trim()) return 'â€”'
  let clean = title.trim()
  clean = clean.replace(/^[A-Z]{2,6}[-_ ]?\d*[A-Za-z]?\s*[-:â€“â€”]\s*/i, '')
  clean = clean.replace(/^[A-Z]{2,6}[-_ ]?\d*[A-Za-z]?\s+/i, '')
  return clean || title
}

/**
 * Converts long facility names to short institutional facility codes.
 * E.g. "Chemistry Laboratory" -> "Chem-Lab", "Physics Laboratory" -> "Phys-Lab", "Computer Lab" -> "Com-Lab"
 */
export function formatFacilityShortCode(facility?: string | null): string {
  if (!facility || !facility.trim()) return 'â€”'
  const lower = facility.toLowerCase()
  if (lower.includes('comp') || lower.includes('computer')) return 'Com-Lab'
  if (lower.includes('chem') || lower.includes('chemistry')) return 'Chem-Lab'
  if (lower.includes('phys') || lower.includes('physics')) return 'Phys-Lab'
  if (lower.includes('bio') || lower.includes('biology')) return 'Bio-Lab'
  if (lower.includes('elec') || lower.includes('electronics')) return 'Elec-Lab'
  return facility.replace(/\s+(Laboratory|Lab).*$/i, '-Lab')
}

'use server'

import { revalidatePath } from 'next/cache'
import { createClient } from '@/lib/supabase/server'
import type { Database } from '@/types/database'
import { isSupabaseConfigured } from '@/lib/supabase/config'
import { recordAuditEvent } from '@/lib/audit'
import { getPgPool } from '@/lib/db'
import { getServerUserScope } from '@/lib/context/server-user-scope'
import { computeCombinedTimeRange } from '@/lib/master-data'
import { parseSlotTimeRange, sortSessionsChronologically } from '@/lib/utils'
import { getSlotRange, slotRangesOverlap as rangesOverlap } from '@/lib/scheduling/conflict-engine'

function cleanMergedSubjectName(name?: string, fallbackPrimary?: string, fallbackSecondary?: string): string {
  if (name) {
    const trimmed = name.trim()
    const parts = trimmed.split(/\s*(?:&|\+)\s*/).map((p) => p.trim()).filter(Boolean)
    if (parts.length > 1 && new Set(parts).size === 1) {
      return parts[0]
    }
    return trimmed
  }
  if (fallbackPrimary && fallbackSecondary) {
    if (fallbackPrimary.trim() === fallbackSecondary.trim()) {
      return fallbackPrimary.trim()
    }
    return `${fallbackPrimary.trim()} & ${fallbackSecondary.trim()}`
  }
  return fallbackPrimary || ''
}

type ScheduleInsert = Database['public']['Tables']['schedules']['Insert']
type LabRow = Database['public']['Tables']['labs']['Row']

// 1. Fetch Active Labs
export async function getLabs(): Promise<LabRow[]> {
  try {
    const pool = getPgPool()
    const res = await pool.query(`
      SELECT id, name, code, capacity, type, status, is_active
      FROM public.labs
      WHERE is_active = true
      ORDER BY name ASC;
    `)
    return (res.rows as LabRow[]) || []
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to get labs:', e)
    return []
  }
}

// 2. Fetch Schedules with Dynamic Teacher Inheritance
export async function getSchedules(filters?: { dayKey?: string; labId?: string }) {
  try {
    const pool = getPgPool()
    let query = `
      SELECT 
        s.*,
        json_build_object('id', l.id, 'name', l.name, 'code', l.code, 'type', l.type) as labs,
        CASE 
          WHEN COALESCE(sp.id, csp.id) IS NOT NULL THEN
            json_build_object('id', COALESCE(sp.id, csp.id), 'full_name', COALESCE(sp.full_name, csp.full_name))
          ELSE NULL 
        END as profiles,
        COALESCE(sp.id, csp.id) as resolved_teacher_id,
        COALESCE(sp.full_name, csp.full_name) as teacher_name
      FROM public.schedules s
      LEFT JOIN public.labs l ON l.id = s.lab_id
      LEFT JOIN public.profiles sp ON sp.id = s.teacher_id
      LEFT JOIN public.class_subjects cs ON cs.id = s.class_subject_id
      LEFT JOIN public.profiles csp ON csp.id = cs.teacher_id
      WHERE 1=1
    `
    const params: any[] = []

    if (filters?.dayKey) {
      params.push(filters.dayKey)
      query += ` AND s.day_key = $${params.length}`
    }

    if (filters?.labId && filters.labId !== 'all') {
      params.push(filters.labId)
      query += ` AND s.lab_id = $${params.length}`
    }

    query += ` ORDER BY s.start_time ASC;`

    const res = await pool.query(query, params)
    return sortSessionsChronologically((res.rows as any[]) || [])
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to get schedules:', e)
    return []
  }
}

// 3. Create / Book a Schedule Slot with Conflict Checking
export async function createSchedule(data: {
  id?: string
  lab_id: string
  teacher_id?: string | null
  subject_name: string
  batch_name: string
  day_key: string
  slot_id: string
  span?: number
  start_time: string
  end_time: string
  status?: string
  class_subject_id?: string | null
  academic_year_id?: string | null
  specific_date?: string | null
  metadata?: any
}) {
  const userScope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (userScope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { error: 'Permission denied: Your account is pending verification and not approved for scheduling operations.' }
  }

  if (!isSupabaseConfigured()) {
    revalidatePath('/schedules')
    revalidatePath('/')
    return { success: true }
  }

  try {
    const supabase = await createClient()

    // Validate target lab status server-side (blocking booking if Under Maintenance or Inactive)
    const { data: targetLab } = await (supabase
      .from('labs') as any)
      .select('id, name, status, is_active')
      .eq('id', data.lab_id)
      .maybeSingle()

    if (targetLab) {
      if (targetLab.status === 'Under Maintenance') {
        return {
          error: `Booking Rejected: "${targetLab.name || data.lab_id}" is currently Under Maintenance and cannot be booked for practical sessions.`,
        }
      }
      if (!targetLab.is_active || targetLab.status === 'Inactive') {
        return {
          error: `Booking Rejected: "${targetLab.name || data.lab_id}" is currently Inactive / Decommissioned.`,
        }
      }
    }

    const secondaryLabId = data.metadata?.secondary_lab_id || data.metadata?.secondaryLabKey
    if (secondaryLabId) {
      if (secondaryLabId === data.lab_id) {
        return {
          error: 'Booking Rejected: Primary and secondary laboratories cannot be the same facility.',
        }
      }
      const { data: secLab } = await (supabase
        .from('labs') as any)
        .select('id, name, status, is_active')
        .eq('id', secondaryLabId)
        .maybeSingle()

      if (secLab) {
        if (secLab.status === 'Under Maintenance') {
          return {
            error: `Booking Rejected: Secondary laboratory "${secLab.name || secondaryLabId}" is currently Under Maintenance and cannot be booked for practical sessions.`,
          }
        }
        if (!secLab.is_active || secLab.status === 'Inactive') {
          return {
            error: `Booking Rejected: Secondary laboratory "${secLab.name || secondaryLabId}" is currently Inactive / Decommissioned.`,
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // Resolve class_subject_id and dual-write display snapshots
    let resolvedClassSubjectId = data.class_subject_id || null
    let targetClassId: string | null = null
    let resolvedAcademicYearId = data.academic_year_id || null
    let displaySubjectName = data.subject_name
    let displayBatchName = data.batch_name
    let resolvedTeacherId = data.teacher_id || null

    if (resolvedClassSubjectId) {
      const { data: cs } = await supabase
        .from('class_subjects')
        .select(`
          id, class_id, subject_id, teacher_id, academic_year_id,
          classes:class_id ( id, name ),
          subjects:subject_id ( id, code, name )
        `)
        .eq('id', resolvedClassSubjectId)
        .maybeSingle()

      if (cs) {
        targetClassId = (cs as any).class_id
        resolvedAcademicYearId = (cs as any).academic_year_id
        if (!resolvedTeacherId) resolvedTeacherId = (cs as any).teacher_id
        const sub = (cs as any).subjects
        const cls = (cs as any).classes
        if (sub?.code && sub?.name) {
          displaySubjectName = `${sub.code} - ${sub.name}`
        }
        if (cls?.name) {
          displayBatchName = cls.name
        }
      }
    } else {
      // Look up candidate class_subjects with strict relational matching (NO loose grade fuzzy-matching)
      const norm = (s?: string | null) => (s ? s.trim().toLowerCase() : '')
      const { data: csList } = await supabase
        .from('class_subjects')
        .select(`
          id, class_id, subject_id, teacher_id, academic_year_id,
          classes:class_id ( id, name, grade ),
          subjects:subject_id ( id, code, name )
        `)

      if (csList && csList.length > 0) {
        const normBatch = norm(data.batch_name)
        const normSub = norm(data.subject_name)
        const cleanBatch = normBatch.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '')
        const cleanSub = normSub.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '')

        const matched = (csList as any[]).find((cs) => {
          const clsName = norm(cs.classes?.name)
          const clsId = norm(cs.class_id)
          const subCode = norm(cs.subjects?.code)
          const subName = norm(cs.subjects?.name)
          const cleanClsName = clsName.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '')
          const cleanClsId = clsId.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '')
          const cleanSubCode = subCode.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, '')

          // Strict alphanumeric class equality or exact prefix match (never match across sections/streams)
          const classMatch =
            normBatch === clsName ||
            normBatch === clsId ||
            (cleanBatch.length > 0 && cleanBatch === cleanClsName) ||
            (cleanBatch.length > 0 && cleanBatch === cleanClsId) ||
            (cleanClsName.length > 1 && cleanBatch.startsWith(cleanClsName))

          // Subject code or full subject title match
          const subjectMatch =
            (cleanSubCode.length > 0 && cleanSub.includes(cleanSubCode)) ||
            (subName.length > 0 && normSub.includes(subName))

          return classMatch && subjectMatch
        })

        if (matched) {
          resolvedClassSubjectId = matched.id
          targetClassId = matched.class_id
          resolvedAcademicYearId = matched.academic_year_id
          if (!resolvedTeacherId) resolvedTeacherId = matched.teacher_id
          const sub = matched.subjects
          const cls = matched.classes
          if (sub?.code && sub?.name) {
            displaySubjectName = `${sub.code} - ${sub.name}`
          }
          if (cls?.name && !displayBatchName) {
            displayBatchName = cls.name
          }
        }
      }
    }

    // 3-Way Multi-Period Span Conflict Detection (Room, Teacher, Cohort)
    const targetRange = getSlotRange(data.slot_id, data.span || 1)

    const { data: daySchedules } = await supabase
      .from('schedules')
      .select('id, lab_id, teacher_id, subject_name, batch_name, slot_id, span, metadata, specific_date, class_subject_id, class_subjects(class_id)')
      .eq('day_key', data.day_key)
      .neq('status', 'cancelled')

    const activeDaySchedules = (daySchedules as any[]) || []
    for (const existing of activeDaySchedules) {
      if (data.id && existing.id === data.id) {
        continue;
      }
      if (existing.specific_date && data.specific_date) {
        const existDateStr = String(existing.specific_date).split('T')[0];
        const reqDateStr = String(data.specific_date).split('T')[0];
        if (existDateStr !== reqDateStr) {
          continue; // Different calendar dates do not collide
        }
      }
      const existRange = getSlotRange(existing.slot_id || 't1', existing.span || 1)
      if (rangesOverlap(targetRange, existRange)) {
        const existSecLabId = existing.metadata?.secondary_lab_id || existing.metadata?.secondaryLabKey
        // 1. Room / Facility Collision (Primary or Secondary lab)
        const primaryCollides = existing.lab_id === data.lab_id || existSecLabId === data.lab_id
        const secondaryCollides = secondaryLabId && (existing.lab_id === secondaryLabId || existSecLabId === secondaryLabId)
        if (primaryCollides || secondaryCollides) {
          const conflictingLab = primaryCollides ? data.lab_id : secondaryLabId
          return {
            error: `Facility Collision: Laboratory (${conflictingLab}) is already reserved for "${existing.subject_name}" (${existing.batch_name}) during Period ${existing.slot_id?.toUpperCase() || ''}.`,
          }
        }
        // 2. Faculty / Teacher Collision
        const sameTeacherId = resolvedTeacherId && existing.teacher_id && resolvedTeacherId === existing.teacher_id
        const reqTeacherName = data.metadata?.teacher?.trim()?.toLowerCase()
        const existTeacherName = existing.metadata?.teacher?.trim()?.toLowerCase()
        const sameTeacherName = reqTeacherName && existTeacherName && reqTeacherName === existTeacherName
        if (sameTeacherId || sameTeacherName) {
          return {
            error: `Faculty Collision: ${data.metadata?.teacher || 'The assigned teacher'} is already conducting "${existing.subject_name}" in another laboratory during this period.`,
          }
        }
        // 3. Student Class Batch Collision (Exact Cohort Comparison - No Substring False Positives)
        const existClassId = (existing.class_subjects as any)?.class_id
        if (targetClassId && existClassId && targetClassId === existClassId) {
          return {
            error: `Class Batch Collision: Student cohort "${displayBatchName || data.batch_name}" is already scheduled for "${existing.subject_name}" in another lab during this period.`,
          }
        } else if (!targetClassId || !existClassId) {
          const reqBatch = (displayBatchName || data.batch_name)?.trim()?.toLowerCase()
          const existBatch = existing.batch_name?.trim()?.toLowerCase()
          if (reqBatch && existBatch && reqBatch === existBatch) {
            return {
              error: `Class Batch Collision: Student group "${data.batch_name}" is already scheduled for "${existing.subject_name}" in another lab during this period.`,
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }

    const VALID_PG_SESSION_STATUSES = new Set(['scheduled', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'cancelled'])
    const rawPgStatus = (data as any).status
    const pgStatus: 'scheduled' | 'in_progress' | 'completed' | 'cancelled' =
      VALID_PG_SESSION_STATUSES.has(rawPgStatus) ? rawPgStatus : 'scheduled'

    const uiStatus = data.metadata?.status || ((data as any).status === 'requested' ? 'requested' : 'confirmed')

    const effectiveSpan = data.span || 1
    let startTime = data.start_time
    let endTime = data.end_time
    if (effectiveSpan > 1 && data.slot_id) {
      const combined = computeCombinedTimeRange(data.slot_id, effectiveSpan)
      const parsed = parseSlotTimeRange(combined)
      if (parsed.startTime) startTime = parsed.startTime
      if (parsed.endTime) endTime = parsed.endTime
    }

    const mergedMetadata = {
      ...(data.metadata || {}),
      status: uiStatus,
      is_adhoc: Boolean(data.specific_date || data.metadata?.is_adhoc),
    }

    const payload: ScheduleInsert = {
      lab_id: data.lab_id,
      teacher_id: resolvedTeacherId,
      subject_name: displaySubjectName,
      batch_name: displayBatchName,
      day_key: data.day_key,
      slot_id: data.slot_id,
      span: effectiveSpan,
      start_time: startTime,
      end_time: endTime,
      is_merged: effectiveSpan > 1,
      status: pgStatus,
      specific_date: data.specific_date || null,
      metadata: mergedMetadata,
      class_subject_id: resolvedClassSubjectId,
      academic_year_id: resolvedAcademicYearId,
    }

    const { data: newSchedule, error } = await (supabase
      .from('schedules') as any)
      .insert(payload)
      .select()
      .single()

    if (error) {
      return { error: error.message }
    }

    // If a teacher submits a slot request, notify Super Admin & Lab Incharge
    if (uiStatus === 'requested') {
      const teacherName = data.metadata?.teacher || 'Subject Teacher'
      const notifId1 = `notif-slot-req-${Date.now()}-admin`
      const notifId2 = `notif-slot-req-${Date.now()}-incharge`
      try {
        const pool = getPgPool()
        await pool.query(
          `INSERT INTO public.lab_notifications (id, target_role, title, message, severity, is_read, created_at)
           VALUES 
             ($1, 'super_admin', $2, $3, 'info', false, NOW()),
             ($4, 'lab_incharge', $5, $6, 'info', false, NOW())
           ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;`,
          [
            notifId1,
            `Slot Booking Request: ${teacherName}`,
            `${teacherName} has requested a practical session for "${data.subject_name}" (${data.batch_name}) on ${data.day_key.toUpperCase()} Period ${data.slot_id.toUpperCase()}.`,
            notifId2,
            `Slot Booking Request: ${teacherName}`,
            `${teacherName} has requested a practical session for "${data.subject_name}" (${data.batch_name}) on ${data.day_key.toUpperCase()} Period ${data.slot_id.toUpperCase()}.`,
          ]
        )
      } catch (notifErr) {
        console.warn('Could not insert slot request notifications:', notifErr)
      }
    }

    revalidatePath('/schedules')
    revalidatePath('/')

    await recordAuditEvent({
      action: 'CREATE',
      entityType: 'schedule',
      entityId: newSchedule?.id || data.slot_id,
      entityLabel: `${data.subject_name} (${data.batch_name}) â€¢ ${data.day_key.toUpperCase()} ${data.slot_id.toUpperCase()}`,
      after: {
        lab_id: data.lab_id,
        teacher_id: data.teacher_id,
        subject_name: data.subject_name,
        batch_name: data.batch_name,
        day_key: data.day_key,
        slot_id: data.slot_id,
        start_time: data.start_time,
        end_time: data.end_time,
        is_multi_lab: Boolean(data.metadata?.is_multi_lab || data.metadata?.secondary_lab_id),
        secondary_lab_id: data.metadata?.secondary_lab_id || null,
      },
      metadata: { operation: 'create_schedule_slot' },
    })

    return { success: true, schedule: newSchedule }
  } catch (e) {
    return { success: true }
  }
}

// 4. Merge Consecutive Schedules
export async function mergeSchedules(
  scheduleId: string,
  nextScheduleId?: string,
  mergedData?: {
    subject_name?: string
    batch_name?: string
    teacher_id?: string
  }
) {
  const userScope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (userScope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { error: 'Permission denied: Your account is not approved for session operations.' }
  }

  if (!isSupabaseConfigured()) {
    revalidatePath('/schedules')
    revalidatePath('/')
    return { success: true }
  }

  try {
    const supabase = await createClient()

    const { data: primary } = await supabase
      .from('schedules')
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', scheduleId)
      .single()

    if (!primary) {
      return { error: 'Primary schedule not found' }
    }

    const primaryObj = primary as any

    if (nextScheduleId) {
      const { data: secondary } = await supabase
        .from('schedules')
        .select('*')
        .eq('id', nextScheduleId)
        .single()

      if (secondary) {
        const secondaryObj = secondary as any
        const combinedSpan = (primaryObj.span || 1) + (secondaryObj.span || 1)
        const computedTimeRange = computeCombinedTimeRange(primaryObj.slot_id, combinedSpan)
        const newEndTime = secondaryObj.end_time || parseSlotTimeRange(computedTimeRange).endTime
        const finalSubjectName = cleanMergedSubjectName(
          mergedData?.subject_name,
          primaryObj.subject_name,
          secondaryObj.subject_name
        )

        // Update primary schedule to merged state with combined span and correct end_time
        const { error: updateError } = await (supabase
          .from('schedules') as any)
          .update({
            is_merged: true,
            span: combinedSpan,
            end_time: newEndTime,
            subject_name: finalSubjectName,
            batch_name: mergedData?.batch_name || primaryObj.batch_name,
            teacher_id: mergedData?.teacher_id || primaryObj.teacher_id,
            metadata: {
              ...(primaryObj.metadata || {}),
              merged_with: secondaryObj.id,
              original_parts: [primaryObj, secondaryObj],
            },
          })
          .eq('id', scheduleId)

        if (updateError) return { error: updateError.message }

        // Delete secondary slot (preserved in metadata.original_parts)
        await supabase.from('schedules').delete().eq('id', nextScheduleId)

        await recordAuditEvent({
          action: 'UPDATE',
          entityType: 'schedule',
          entityId: scheduleId,
          entityLabel: `Merged Slot: ${finalSubjectName}`,
          before: {
            is_merged: false,
            span: primaryObj.span || 1,
            end_time: primaryObj.end_time,
            subject_name: primaryObj.subject_name,
          },
          after: {
            is_merged: true,
            span: combinedSpan,
            end_time: newEndTime,
            subject_name: finalSubjectName,
          },
          metadata: { operation: 'merge_schedules', nextScheduleId },
        })
      }
    } else {
      // Extend single slot
      const newSpan = (primaryObj.span || 1) + 1
      const computedTimeRange = computeCombinedTimeRange(primaryObj.slot_id, newSpan)
      const newEndTime = parseSlotTimeRange(computedTimeRange).endTime
      await (supabase
        .from('schedules') as any)
        .update({
          is_merged: true,
          span: newSpan,
          end_time: newEndTime,
        })
        .eq('id', scheduleId)

      await recordAuditEvent({
        action: 'UPDATE',
        entityType: 'schedule',
        entityId: scheduleId,
        entityLabel: `Extended Duration: ${primaryObj.subject_name}`,
        before: { span: primaryObj.span || 1, end_time: primaryObj.end_time },
        after: { span: newSpan, end_time: newEndTime },
        metadata: { operation: 'extend_schedule_span' },
      })
    }

    revalidatePath('/schedules')
    revalidatePath('/')
    return { success: true }
  } catch (e) {
    return { success: true }
  }
}

// 5. Unmerge / Split Session
export async function unmergeSchedule(scheduleId: string) {
  const userScope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (userScope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { error: 'Permission denied: Your account is not approved for session operations.' }
  }

  if (!isSupabaseConfigured()) {
    revalidatePath('/schedules')
    revalidatePath('/')
    return { success: true }
  }

  try {
    const supabase = await createClient()

    const { data: schedule } = await supabase
      .from('schedules')
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', scheduleId)
      .single()

    if (!schedule) return { error: 'Schedule not found' }

    const scheduleObj = schedule as any
    const originalParts = scheduleObj.metadata?.original_parts || []
    const primaryOriginal = originalParts[0]
    const secondaryOriginal = originalParts[1]

    const restoredEndTime =
      primaryOriginal?.end_time || parseSlotTimeRange(computeCombinedTimeRange(scheduleObj.slot_id, 1)).endTime
    const restoredSubjectName =
      primaryOriginal?.subject_name || scheduleObj.subject_name.split(' & ')[0].split(' + ')[0].trim()
    const restoredBatchName =
      primaryOriginal?.batch_name || scheduleObj.batch_name.split(' & ')[0].trim()

    // Restore primary schedule
    await (supabase
      .from('schedules') as any)
      .update({
        is_merged: false,
        span: 1,
        end_time: restoredEndTime,
        subject_name: restoredSubjectName,
        batch_name: restoredBatchName,
        metadata: {
          ...(scheduleObj.metadata || {}),
          merged_with: null,
          original_parts: null,
        },
      })
      .eq('id', scheduleId)

    // Restore secondary schedule if it existed
    if (secondaryOriginal) {
      await supabase.from('schedules').insert({
        id: secondaryOriginal.id,
        lab_id: secondaryOriginal.lab_id,
        teacher_id: secondaryOriginal.teacher_id,
        subject_name: secondaryOriginal.subject_name,
        batch_name: secondaryOriginal.batch_name,
        start_time: secondaryOriginal.start_time,
        end_time: secondaryOriginal.end_time,
        slot_id: secondaryOriginal.slot_id,
        day_key: secondaryOriginal.day_key,
        span: secondaryOriginal.span || 1,
        is_merged: false,
        status: secondaryOriginal.status || 'scheduled',
        metadata: secondaryOriginal.metadata || {},
        class_subject_id: secondaryOriginal.class_subject_id || null,
        academic_year_id: secondaryOriginal.academic_year_id || null,
      })
    }

    await recordAuditEvent({
      action: 'UPDATE',
      entityType: 'schedule',
      entityId: scheduleId,
      entityLabel: `Split / Unmerged: ${scheduleObj.subject_name}`,
      before: { is_merged: true, span: scheduleObj.span || 2, end_time: scheduleObj.end_time },
      after: { is_merged: false, span: 1, end_time: restoredEndTime },
      metadata: { operation: 'unmerge_schedule', restoredSecondaryId: secondaryOriginal?.id },
    })

    revalidatePath('/schedules')
    revalidatePath('/')
    return { success: true }
  } catch (e) {
    return { success: true }
  }
}

// 6. Delete / Cancel Schedule
export async function deleteSchedule(scheduleId: string): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }> {
  const userScope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (userScope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { success: false, error: 'Permission denied: Your account is pending verification and not approved for schedule deletion.' }
  }

  if (!isSupabaseConfigured()) {
    revalidatePath('/schedules')
    revalidatePath('/admin')
    revalidatePath('/')
    return { success: true }
  }

  try {
    const supabase = await createClient()

    // 1. Fetch pre-state for audit record
    const { data: beforeData } = await (supabase.from('schedules') as any)
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', scheduleId)
      .maybeSingle()

    // 2. Perform DB deletion
    const { error, count } = await (supabase.from('schedules') as any)
      .delete({ count: 'exact' })
      .eq('id', scheduleId)

    if (error) {
      console.error('[DATABASE ERROR] Delete schedule failed:', error)
      return { success: false, error: error.message }
    }

    if (count === 0) {
      console.warn('[DATABASE WARNING] No row was deleted from public.schedules for ID:', scheduleId)
    }

    revalidatePath('/schedules')
    revalidatePath('/admin')
    revalidatePath('/')

    // 3. Record DELETE audit event
    await recordAuditEvent({
      action: 'DELETE',
      entityType: 'schedule',
      entityId: scheduleId,
      entityLabel: beforeData?.subject_name
        ? `${beforeData.subject_name} â€¢ ${(beforeData.day_key || '').toUpperCase()}`
        : `Timetable Slot #${scheduleId}`,
      before: beforeData || { schedule_id: scheduleId },
      metadata: { operation: 'delete_schedule_slot' },
    })

    return { success: true }
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.error('[DATABASE ERROR] Delete schedule exception:', e)
    return { success: false, error: e?.message || 'Failed to delete schedule' }
  }
}

// 6b. Reset Schedules to Authoritative Master Routine
export async function resetSchedulesToMaster(): Promise<{ success: boolean; count?: number; error?: string }> {
  const userScope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (userScope.role !== 'super_admin' || userScope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { success: false, error: 'Unauthorized: Resetting master schedules is restricted to approved Super Administrators.' }
  }

  try {
    const pool = getPgPool()
    const client = await pool.connect()

    try {
      await client.query('BEGIN')

      // Ensure all 5 standard labs exist in public.labs
      const standardLabs = [
        { id: 'comp', name: 'Computer Engineering Lab 01', code: 'LAB-COMP-01', type: 'computer_lab', capacity: 40 },
        { id: 'phys', name: 'Physics Laboratory', code: 'LAB-PHYS-01', type: 'physics_lab', capacity: 38 },
        { id: 'chem', name: 'Chemistry Laboratory', code: 'LAB-CHEM-01', type: 'chemistry_lab', capacity: 40 },
        { id: 'bio', name: 'Biology & Life Sciences Lab', code: 'LAB-BIO-01', type: 'biology_lab', capacity: 35 },
        { id: 'elec', name: 'Electronics & Hardware Lab', code: 'LAB-ELEC-01', type: 'electronics_lab', capacity: 30 },
      ]
      for (const lab of standardLabs) {
        await client.query(`
          INSERT INTO public.labs (id, name, code, type, capacity, status, is_active)
          VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, 'Operational', true)
          ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;
        `, [lab.id, lab.name, lab.code, lab.type, lab.capacity])
      }

      // Reset all schedules to default 'scheduled' status and clear skipped metadata
      const updateRes = await client.query(`
        UPDATE public.schedules
        SET status = 'scheduled',
            metadata = jsonb_set(
              COALESCE(metadata, '{}'::jsonb),
              '{is_skipped}',
              'false'::jsonb
            );
      `)

      await client.query('COMMIT')
    } catch (err) {
      await client.query('ROLLBACK')
      throw err
    } finally {
      client.release()
    }

    await recordAuditEvent({
      action: 'UPDATE',
      entityType: 'policy',
      entityId: 'master_routine_reset',
      entityLabel: 'Master Timetable Schedule Reset',
      before: { status: 'custom_modified' },
      after: { status: 'restored_to_master_baseline' },
      metadata: { operation: 'reset_schedules_to_master' },
    })

    revalidatePath('/schedules')
    revalidatePath('/admin')
    revalidatePath('/')

    return { success: true }
  } catch (err: any) {
    console.error('[DATABASE ERROR] Reset schedules to master failed:', err)
    return { success: false, error: err?.message || 'Failed to reset schedules.' }
  }
}

/**
 * Update an existing scheduled slot in PostgreSQL.
 * Fetches pre-state, applies changes, and records UPDATE audit event with structured diff.
 */
export async function updateSchedule(
  scheduleId: string,
  data: {
    lab_id?: string
    teacher_id?: string | null
    subject_name?: string
    batch_name?: string
    day_key?: string
    slot_id?: string
    span?: number
    start_time?: string
    end_time?: string
    class_subject_id?: string | null
    academic_year_id?: string | null
    status?: 'confirmed' | 'requested' | 'skipped' | 'scheduled'
    metadata?: any
  }
): Promise<{ success: boolean; schedule?: any; error?: string }> {
  const userScope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (userScope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { success: false, error: 'Permission denied: Your account is not approved for timetable modifications.' }
  }

  if (!isSupabaseConfigured()) {
    revalidatePath('/schedules')
    revalidatePath('/')
    return { success: true }
  }

  try {
    const supabase = await createClient()

    // 1. Fetch before state
    const { data: beforeSchedule } = await (supabase
      .from('schedules') as any)
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', scheduleId)
      .maybeSingle()

    // 2. Perform DB update
    const updatePayload: any = {}
    if (data.lab_id !== undefined) updatePayload.lab_id = data.lab_id
    if (data.teacher_id !== undefined) updatePayload.teacher_id = data.teacher_id
    if (data.subject_name !== undefined) updatePayload.subject_name = data.subject_name
    if (data.batch_name !== undefined) updatePayload.batch_name = data.batch_name
    if (data.day_key !== undefined) updatePayload.day_key = data.day_key
    if (data.slot_id !== undefined) updatePayload.slot_id = data.slot_id
    if (data.span !== undefined) updatePayload.span = data.span
    if (data.start_time !== undefined) updatePayload.start_time = data.start_time
    if (data.end_time !== undefined) updatePayload.end_time = data.end_time
    if (data.status !== undefined) {
      const VALID_PG_SESSION_STATUSES = new Set(['scheduled', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'cancelled'])
      updatePayload.status = VALID_PG_SESSION_STATUSES.has(data.status as any) ? data.status : 'scheduled'
      updatePayload.metadata = {
        ...(updatePayload.metadata || beforeSchedule?.metadata || {}),
        status: data.status,
      }
    }
    if (data.metadata !== undefined) updatePayload.metadata = data.metadata

    if (data.class_subject_id !== undefined) {
      updatePayload.class_subject_id = data.class_subject_id
      if (data.class_subject_id) {
        const { data: cs } = await supabase
          .from('class_subjects')
          .select('id, academic_year_id, classes:class_id(name), subjects:subject_id(code, name)')
          .eq('id', data.class_subject_id)
          .maybeSingle()
        if (cs) {
          if (data.subject_name === undefined && (cs as any).subjects?.code) {
            updatePayload.subject_name = `${(cs as any).subjects.code} - ${(cs as any).subjects.name}`
          }
          if (data.batch_name === undefined && (cs as any).classes?.name) {
            updatePayload.batch_name = (cs as any).classes.name
          }
          if (data.academic_year_id === undefined && (cs as any).academic_year_id) {
            updatePayload.academic_year_id = (cs as any).academic_year_id
          }
        }
      }
    }
    if (data.academic_year_id !== undefined) updatePayload.academic_year_id = data.academic_year_id

    let { data: updatedSchedule, error } = await (supabase
      .from('schedules') as any)
      .update(updatePayload)
      .eq('id', scheduleId)
      .select()
      .maybeSingle()

    // ðŸ›¡ï¸ Fallback: If slot was a default preset not yet saved to PostgreSQL, upsert it!
    if (!beforeSchedule && !updatedSchedule && !error) {
      const upsertPayload = {
        id: scheduleId,
        lab_id: data.lab_id || 'comp',
        teacher_id: data.teacher_id || null,
        subject_name: updatePayload.subject_name || data.subject_name || 'Practical Session',
        batch_name: updatePayload.batch_name || data.batch_name || 'Class 12',
        day_key: data.day_key || 'sun',
        slot_id: data.slot_id || 't1',
        span: data.span || 1,
        start_time: data.start_time || '10:10',
        end_time: data.end_time || '11:00',
        status: data.status || 'scheduled',
        metadata: data.metadata || {},
        class_subject_id: updatePayload.class_subject_id || null,
        academic_year_id: updatePayload.academic_year_id || null,
      }
      const upsertRes = await (supabase
        .from('schedules') as any)
        .upsert(upsertPayload)
        .select()
        .maybeSingle()

      if (!upsertRes.error && upsertRes.data) {
        updatedSchedule = upsertRes.data
      }
    }

    if (error) {
      console.error('[LMR] Error updating schedule:', error)
      return { success: false, error: error.message }
    }

    const safeBefore = beforeSchedule || {
      id: scheduleId,
      subject_name: data.subject_name ? 'Default Timetable Slot' : 'Practical Session',
      slot_id: data.slot_id || 't1',
      day_key: data.day_key || 'sun',
    }
    const safeAfter = updatedSchedule || {
      ...safeBefore,
      ...updatePayload,
    }

    // 3. Record UPDATE audit event with structured diff
    await recordAuditEvent({
      action: 'UPDATE',
      entityType: 'schedule',
      entityId: scheduleId,
      entityLabel: `${data.subject_name || safeBefore?.subject_name || 'Timetable Slot'} â€¢ ${(data.day_key || safeBefore?.day_key || '').toUpperCase()}`,
      before: safeBefore,
      after: safeAfter,
      metadata: { operation: 'update_schedule_slot' },
    })

    revalidatePath('/schedules')
    revalidatePath('/')
    return { success: true, schedule: updatedSchedule }
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.error('Failed to update schedule:', e)
    return { success: false, error: e?.message || 'Failed to update schedule' }
  }
}

// 7. Update Schedule Status (e.g. 'confirmed', 'requested', 'skipped', with decline reason)
export async function updateScheduleStatus(scheduleId: string, data: {
  status: 'confirmed' | 'requested' | 'skipped' | 'scheduled'
  is_skipped?: boolean
  skipped_reason?: string
  skipped_by?: string
  decline_reason?: string
}) {
  const userScope = await getServerUserScope()
  if (userScope.approvalStatus !== 'approved') {
    return { success: false, error: 'Permission denied: Your account is not approved for session status changes.' }
  }

  if (!isSupabaseConfigured()) {
    revalidatePath('/schedules')
    revalidatePath('/')
    return { success: true }
  }

  try {
    const supabase = await createClient()

    const { data: existing } = await (supabase.from('schedules') as any)
      .select('*')
      .eq('id', scheduleId)
      .single()

    const currentMeta = (existing as any)?.metadata || {}
    const updatedMeta = {
      ...currentMeta,
      is_skipped: data.is_skipped ?? (data.status === 'skipped'),
      skipped_reason: data.skipped_reason || currentMeta.skipped_reason,
      skipped_by: data.skipped_by || currentMeta.skipped_by,
      decline_reason: data.decline_reason || currentMeta.decline_reason,
    }

    const VALID_PG_SESSION_STATUSES = new Set(['scheduled', 'in_progress', 'completed', 'cancelled'])
    const pgStatus = VALID_PG_SESSION_STATUSES.has(data.status)
      ? data.status
      : (data.status === 'skipped' ? 'cancelled' : 'scheduled')

    const { error } = await (supabase.from('schedules') as any)
      .update({
        status: pgStatus,
        metadata: updatedMeta,
      })
      .eq('id', scheduleId)

    if (error) return { error: error.message }

    const subjectName = (existing as any)?.subject_name || 'Practical Session'

    // Record UPDATE audit event for status change
    await recordAuditEvent({
      action: 'UPDATE',
      entityType: 'schedule',
      entityId: scheduleId,
      entityLabel: `${subjectName} (${data.status.toUpperCase()})`,
      before: {
        status: (existing as any)?.status || 'scheduled',
        is_skipped: currentMeta.is_skipped || false,
      },
      after: {
        status: data.status,
        is_skipped: updatedMeta.is_skipped,
        ...(data.skipped_reason ? { skipped_reason: data.skipped_reason } : {}),
        ...(data.decline_reason ? { decline_reason: data.decline_reason } : {}),
      },
      metadata: { operation: 'update_schedule_status' },
    })

    // If declined with reason, notify the teacher
    if (data.status === 'skipped' && data.decline_reason && currentMeta.teacher) {
      const notifId = `notif-slot-dec-${Date.now()}`
      try {
        const pool = getPgPool()
        await pool.query(
          `INSERT INTO public.lab_notifications (id, target_role, title, message, severity, is_read, created_at)
           VALUES ($1, 'teacher', $2, $3, 'warning', false, NOW())
           ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;`,
          [
            notifId,
            `Slot Request Declined: ${subjectName}`,
            `Your booking request was declined. Reason: "${data.decline_reason}"`,
          ]
        )
      } catch (notifErr) {
        console.warn('Could not insert slot decline notification:', notifErr)
      }
    }

    // If approved, notify the teacher
    if (data.status === 'confirmed' && currentMeta.teacher) {
      const notifId = `notif-slot-app-${Date.now()}`
      try {
        const pool = getPgPool()
        await pool.query(
          `INSERT INTO public.lab_notifications (id, target_role, title, message, severity, is_read, created_at)
           VALUES ($1, 'teacher', $2, $3, 'info', false, NOW())
           ON CONFLICT (id) DO NOTHING;`,
          [
            notifId,
            `Slot Request Approved: ${subjectName}`,
            `Your booking request for ${subjectName} has been approved and confirmed.`,
          ]
        )
      } catch (notifErr) {
        console.warn('Could not insert slot approval notification:', notifErr)
      }
    }

    revalidatePath('/schedules')
    revalidatePath('/')
    return { success: true }
  } catch (e: any) {
    return { error: e.message || 'Failed to update schedule status' }
  }
}


=========================================
FILE: app\(dashboard)\records\new\log-form.tsx
=========================================

'use client'

import { useState, useMemo, useCallback, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { createPracticalLog } from '@/app/actions/logs'
import { getStudentsByClass } from '@/app/actions/students'
import type { StudentWithEnrollment } from '@/lib/types/students'
import { useLogsState } from '@/hooks/use-logs-state'
import { Database } from '@/types/database'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { Select } from '@/components/ui/select'
import { Badge } from '@/components/ui/badge'
import { NepaliDatePicker } from '@/components/ui/nepali-date-picker'
import {
  FileCheck,
  Users,
  Sparkles,
  BookOpen,
  Clock,
  Building2,
  Grid3X3,
  Hash,
  RotateCcw,
  Calendar,
  AlertCircle,
  Info,
  X,
  Loader2,
} from 'lucide-react'
import {
  computeMultiPeriodLabel,
  cleanSubjectTitle,
  formatSubjectHeading,
  formatClassBatchLabel,
  matchesGrade,
} from '@/lib/master-data'
import { useInfrastructureState } from '@/hooks/use-infrastructure-state'

type Lab = Database['public']['Tables']['labs']['Row']
type Teacher = { id: string; full_name: string }

export default function LogForm({
  labs,
  teachers,
  scopedOptions,
  initialClasses,
}: {
  labs: Lab[]
  teachers: Teacher[]
  scopedOptions?: any
  initialClasses?: any[]
}) {
  const router = useRouter()
  const { saveLog } = useLogsState()
  const {
    isLoading: isInfraLoading,
    classes: infraClasses,
    subjects: infraSubjects,
    faculty: infraFaculty,
    labs: infraLabs,
    periods: infraPeriods,
    getHolidayForDate
  } = useInfrastructureState()

  const isTeacher = Boolean(scopedOptions && !scopedOptions.isPrivileged)

  // Live classes pool: prioritize server-provided initialClasses, then live infraClasses
  const rawClasses = useMemo(() => {
    if (initialClasses && initialClasses.length > 0) return initialClasses
    if (infraClasses && infraClasses.length > 0) return infraClasses
    if (isTeacher && scopedOptions?.assignedClasses && scopedOptions.assignedClasses.length > 0) {
      return scopedOptions.assignedClasses.map((clsName: string) => ({
        id: clsName.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g, ''),
        name: clsName,
        section: 'A',
        capacity: 40,
        stream: clsName.includes('11') || clsName.includes('12') ? 'Science' : 'General',
        is_active: true,
      }))
    }
    return []
  }, [initialClasses, infraClasses, isTeacher, scopedOptions])

  // Compute available classes: if non-privileged teacher, strictly filter to their assigned classes
  const availableClasses = useMemo(() => {
    if (isTeacher && scopedOptions?.assignedClasses?.length > 0) {
      return rawClasses.filter((c: any) =>
        scopedOptions.assignedClasses.some((ac: string) => ac === c.name || matchesGrade(ac, c.name))
      )
    }
    return rawClasses
  }, [isTeacher, scopedOptions, rawClasses])

  // Live subjects pool: live infraSubjects from PostgreSQL without hardcoded fallbacks
  const rawSubjects = useMemo(() => {
    if (infraSubjects && infraSubjects.length > 0) return infraSubjects
    if (isTeacher && scopedOptions?.assignedSubjects && scopedOptions.assignedSubjects.length > 0) {
      return scopedOptions.assignedSubjects
    }
    return []
  }, [infraSubjects, isTeacher, scopedOptions])

  // Merged effective labs (server labs + live infra labs)
  const effectiveLabs = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, { id: string; name: string }>()
    labs.forEach((l) => map.set(l.id, { id: l.id, name: l.name }))
    if (infraLabs) {
      infraLabs.forEach((l) => map.set(l.id, { id: l.id, name: l.name }))
    }
    return Array.from(map.values())
  }, [labs, infraLabs])

  // Merged effective teachers (server teachers + live infra faculty)
  const effectiveTeachers = useMemo(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, { id: string; full_name: string }>()
    teachers.forEach((t) => map.set(t.id, { id: t.id, full_name: t.full_name }))
    if (infraFaculty) {
      infraFaculty.forEach((f) => map.set(f.id, { id: f.id, full_name: f.name }))
    }
    return Array.from(map.values())
  }, [teachers, infraFaculty])

  const defaultClass = availableClasses[0] || { id: '11eng', name: '11 Eng', capacity: 40 }
  const [selectedClassId, setSelectedClassId] = useState<string>(defaultClass.id)
  const activeClassObj = availableClasses.find((c: any) => c.id === selectedClassId) || defaultClass

  // Auto-sync selected class if current selection is invalid or when availableClasses loads
  useEffect(() => {
    if (availableClasses.length > 0) {
      const exists = availableClasses.some((c: any) => c.id === selectedClassId)
      if (!exists) {
        setSelectedClassId(availableClasses[0].id)
      }
    }
  }, [availableClasses, selectedClassId])

  const [classStudents, setClassStudents] = useState<StudentWithEnrollment[]>([])

  useEffect(() => {
    let isMounted = true
    getStudentsByClass({ classId: selectedClassId }).then((res) => {
      if (isMounted && res.success && res.data) {
        setClassStudents(res.data)
        if (res.data.length > 0) {
          setTotalStudents(res.data.length)
        }
      }
    })
    return () => {
      isMounted = false
    }
  }, [selectedClassId])

  // Helper to dynamically filter subjects for a class
  const getSubjectsForClassObj = useCallback(
    (clsName: string) => {
      if (!clsName) return rawSubjects
      const direct = rawSubjects.filter((s: any) => s.grade === clsName)
      if (direct.length > 0) return direct
      const fuzzy = rawSubjects.filter((s: any) => matchesGrade(s.grade, clsName))
      if (fuzzy.length > 0) return fuzzy
      return rawSubjects
    },
    [rawSubjects]
  )

  // Filter subjects strictly for the selected class and teacher
  const classSubjects = useMemo(() => {
    if (isTeacher && scopedOptions?.assignedSubjects?.length > 0) {
      const teacherSubs = scopedOptions.assignedSubjects.filter((s: any) =>
        s.grade === activeClassObj?.name || matchesGrade(s.grade, activeClassObj?.name)
      )
      if (teacherSubs.length > 0) return teacherSubs
    }
    return getSubjectsForClassObj(activeClassObj?.name)
  }, [isTeacher, scopedOptions, activeClassObj, getSubjectsForClassObj])

  const initialSub = classSubjects[0] || rawSubjects[0] || { code: 'COMP-12', title: 'Practical Session', labId: 'comp' }

  const [selectedSubjectCode, setSelectedSubjectCode] = useState<string>(initialSub.code)

  // Auto-sync selected subject when class changes or when subjects load
  useEffect(() => {
    if (classSubjects.length > 0) {
      const exists = classSubjects.some((s: any) => s.code === selectedSubjectCode)
      if (!exists) {
        setSelectedSubjectCode(classSubjects[0].code)
        if (classSubjects[0].labId) setSelectedLabId(classSubjects[0].labId)
      }
    }
  }, [classSubjects, selectedSubjectCode])

  // 1. Facility & In-Charge (Auto-synced to subject's designated teacher & room)
  const [selectedLabId, setSelectedLabId] = useState<string>(initialSub.labId || effectiveLabs[0]?.id || 'comp')
  const [selectedTeacherId, setSelectedTeacherId] = useState<string>(
    isTeacher
      ? scopedOptions?.teacher?.id || 't1'
      : initialSub.teacherId || effectiveTeachers[0]?.id || 't1'
  )
  const [isDualLab, setIsDualLab] = useState<boolean>(false)
  const [secondaryLabId, setSecondaryLabId] = useState<string>(
    effectiveLabs.find((l) => l.id !== (initialSub.labId || effectiveLabs[0]?.id || 'comp'))?.id || 'phys'
  )

  // 2. Session Date
  const [sessionDate, setSessionDate] = useState<string>(
    new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]
  )
  const activeHoliday = getHolidayForDate(sessionDate)
  const isHoliday = !!activeHoliday

  // 3. Academic Period Selection (Default: Single period, e.g. first available)
  const [selectedPeriods, setSelectedPeriods] = useState<string[]>(['t1'])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (infraPeriods && infraPeriods.length > 0) {
      const nonBreak = infraPeriods.find(
        (p) => !p.id.toLowerCase().includes('break') && !p.name?.toLowerCase().includes('break')
      )
      if (nonBreak && selectedPeriods.length === 1 && selectedPeriods[0] === 't1' && !infraPeriods.some((p) => p.id === 't1')) {
        setSelectedPeriods([nonBreak.id])
      }
    }
  }, [infraPeriods, selectedPeriods])

  // 5. Attendance State: Roll Grid by default (all students present by default)
  const [attendanceMode, setAttendanceMode] = useState<'grid' | 'counter'>('grid')
  const [totalStudents, setTotalStudents] = useState<number>(
    'strength' in activeClassObj ? (activeClassObj as any).strength : ('students' in activeClassObj ? (activeClassObj as any).students : 40)
  )
  const [absentRolls, setAbsentRolls] = useState<number[]>([])
  const [absentCountInput, setAbsentCountInput] = useState<number>(0)

  const markedAbsentStudents = useMemo(() => {
    return absentRolls.map((roll) => {
      const student = classStudents.find((s) => s.roll_number === roll)
      return {
        roll,
        name: student ? student.full_name : `Roll ${roll}`,
        photo_url: student?.photo_url || null,
      }
    })
  }, [absentRolls, classStudents])

  // 6. Practical Topic & Observations (Starts clean for instructor input)
  const [practicalTitle, setPracticalTitle] = useState<string>('')
  const [remarks, setRemarks] = useState<string>('')

  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [duplicateId, setDuplicateId] = useState<string | null>(null)

  // Turnout calculation & strict clamping (0% to 100% max)
  const effectiveAbsentCount =
    attendanceMode === 'grid'
      ? absentRolls.length
      : Math.min(totalStudents, Math.max(0, absentCountInput))
  const presentStudents = Math.min(totalStudents, Math.max(0, totalStudents - effectiveAbsentCount))
  const attendancePercentage =
    totalStudents > 0
      ? Math.min(100, Math.max(0, Math.round((presentStudents / totalStudents) * 100)))
      : 0

  // Multi-period computed label (uses live infraPeriods for accurate timing calculation)
  const periodInfo = useMemo(() => {
    return computeMultiPeriodLabel(selectedPeriods, infraPeriods)
  }, [selectedPeriods, infraPeriods])

  const togglePeriod = (slotId: string) => {
    if (selectedPeriods.includes(slotId)) {
      if (selectedPeriods.length === 1) return // keep at least 1
